### [75] [Fair Multi-View Determinantal Coresets via Adaptive NEPv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18181)
*Richard Yi Da Xu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出"公平多视角行列式点过程选择"问题：从大候选池中选取小子集时，需同时最大化最弱视角下的对数行列式。作者将该非光滑目标平滑化并松弛到Stiefel流形，提出自适应自洽场(SCF)求解器（含阻尼和电平位移），结合杠杆分数筛选和公平局部细化进行舍入。求解器仅需每视角的特征映射乘积，并在合成数据上验证，同时提出了多模态USPTO协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一DPP核可能在某视角下失效，而平均化核将多视角松弛退化为普通单核谱问题，无法真正平衡多个互不相容的多样性概念（如商标策展中语言描述与视觉空间）。

Method: 1) 形式化公平多视角行列式选择问题：最大化最弱视角log det；2) 平滑非光滑目标并松弛至Stiefel流形；3) 推导规范不变非线性特征值问题的平稳性条件，含特征向量依赖的自适应视角权重；4) 提出带阻尼和电平位移的自适应SCF求解器；5) 通过杠杆分数筛选加公平局部细化进行舍入；6) 仅需各视角的特征映射乘积。

Result: 在冲突视角的合成实验上验证了方法有效性；提出了多模态USPTO协议；真实多模态结果因需对齐的商标嵌入而暂未在本版本中报告。

Conclusion: 公平多视角行列式选择比单视角情形更复杂（无闭式谱解），但通过自适应SCF求解器可以高效处理；真实数据上的验证仍待后续工作推进。

Abstract: Selecting a small, diverse subset from a large candidate pool often means balancing several incompatible notions of diversity. In trademark curation, for instance, a subset should cover both the language used to describe marks and the visual space of their logos. A single determinantal point process (\DPP) kernel can hide failure in one view, and averaging kernels replaces the multi-view relaxation by an ordinary single-kernel spectral problem. We formulate \emph{fair multi-view determinant selection}: maximize the weakest per-view log determinant of a size-$k$ subset. We smooth this nonsmooth objective and relax it to the Stiefel manifold. The relaxation embeds every discrete subset exactly, but unlike its single-view counterpart it has no closed-form spectral solution in general. Its stationarity condition is a gauge-invariant nonlinear eigenvalue problem with eigenvector-dependent, view-adaptive weights. We derive an adaptive self-consistent-field (\SCF) solver with damping and level shifting, and round the resulting subspace by leverage-score screening followed by fair local refinement. The solver needs only feature-map products for each view. We report conflicting-view synthetic experiments and specify a multimodal USPTO protocol; the real-data multimodal results require aligned logo embeddings and are not claimed in this version.

</details>


### [76] [Sobolev Regularized Score Difference Estimation in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18237)
*Chenghan Xie,Jose Blanchet,Renyuan Xu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Sobolev正则化的分数差估计器，用于生成建模中两个Stein分数函数的差值估计，该估计器在统计上具有一致性、可扩展到高维，并证明了收敛率和极小极大下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数差估计在迁移学习和扩散模型后训练（如判别器引导）中至关重要，但现有估计器要么缺乏统计一致性，要么难以扩展到高维场景。

Method: 提出基于Sobolev正则化的分数差估计器，通过正则化确保小样本下的一致性并稳定训练过程。

Result: 建立了收敛率O(n^{-(s-1)/(d+2s-2)})和极小极大下界Ω̃(n^{-2(s-1)/(d+2s)})（均方误差），在ECG信号生成的迁移学习任务中显著优于非正则化方法。

Conclusion: 该估计器在小样本条件下比现有方法具有显著更优的稳定性，并在真实任务（ECG信号生成迁移学习）中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Estimating the difference of two Stein's score functions is a fundamental problem in generative modeling. In particular, score differences arise naturally in transfer learning, where the score difference provides the mechanism for adapting a pre-trained model to a new target distribution, and in diffusion model-based post-training methods such as discriminator guidance. Existing estimators for score differences in these settings either lack of statistical consistency or are difficult to scale up in high-dimensions. We propose a statistically consistent and scalable estimator for score differences based on Sobolev regularization, which plays a crucial role in ensuring consistency and stablizing the training in the small-sample regime. Mathematically, we establish a convergence rate of $O(n^{-\frac{s-1}{d+2s-2}})$ where $d$ is the dimension and $s$ denotes the smoothness of the underlying densities, and provide a minimax lower bound of $\tildeΩ(n^{-\frac{2(s-1)}{d+2s}})$ (in mean-squared error). Empirically, our estimator exhibits significantly improved stability in small-sample regimes compared to existing methods. We demonstrate its effectiveness on real-world tasks, including transfer learning for ECG signal generation, where it substantially outperforms non-regularized score difference estimators in downstream classification performance.

</details>


### [77] [Inference and Uncertainty Quantification for Streaming $r$-PCA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18374)
*Haoshu Xu,Hongzhe Li*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了Oja算法在流式PCA中的两个开放问题：一般秩下子高斯数据的尖锐算子范数收敛性，以及所得子空间估计量的分布推断。本文去除了传统分析中无法消除的余项，获得了尖锐收敛速率，在稠密尾部尖峰协方差结构下达到（对数因子意义的）极小极大速率，并证明了一般下界。通过迭代线性化实现高维高斯逼近，建立了行向高斯逼近，并发展了在线乘子自助法实现实际推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Oja算法的收敛性分析（即使在一秩情形）要么假设数据有界，要么留下非消失的余项，使得无法适应多项式衰减的尾部谱；而现有分布结果仅限于一秩情形。因此需要解决一般秩下的尖锐收敛分析及相应的分布推断问题。

Method: 1) 去除收敛性分析中的余项，得到尖锐收敛速率；2) 推导Oja迭代的线性化表示；3) 利用线性化实现高维高斯逼近，
并给出显式极限协方差；4) 建立对齐差分的行向（convex sets上的）高斯逼近；5) 开发在线乘子自助法（online multiplier bootstrap）并证明其一致性；6) 在稠密尾和稀疏尾两种机制下证明匹配下界（对数因子意义下）。

Result: 在稠密尾部尖峰协方差机制下，收敛速率达到（至多对数因子的）极小极大速率；更一般地，在温和非退化条件下，稠密尾和稀疏尾两种机制都得到匹配下界。建立了一般秩子空间估计误差的高维高斯逼近（显式极限协方差）以及行向高斯逼近（推广一秩结果），并证明在线乘子自助法的一致性。

Conclusion: 本文解决了流式PCA中Oja算法的两个核心开放问题，不仅获得了尖锐的收敛速率和匹配下界，还建立了从高斯逼近到自助法推断的完整分布推断框架。其技术方法（线性化、高斯逼近、自助推断）对更广泛的非凸随机逼近问题也具贡献。

Abstract: We address two open questions in streaming PCA via Oja's algorithm: sharp operator-norm convergence for general rank under sub-Gaussian data, and distributional inference for the resulting subspace estimator. Existing convergence analyses, even in the rank-one case, either assume bounded data or leave non-vanishing remainder terms that prevent adaptation to a polynomially vanishing tail spectrum, while existing distributional results are confined to the rank-one case. Our convergence theory removes these remainder terms and yields a sharp rate. In the dense-tail spiked covariance regime, this rate matches the minimax rate up to logarithmic factors. More generally, we prove a matching lower bound, up to logarithmic factors, across both dense-tail and sparse-tail regimes under a mild nondegeneracy condition. The analysis yields a linearization of Oja's iterates, which in turn enables a high-dimensional Gaussian approximation for the general-rank subspace estimation error with an explicit limiting covariance. We also establish a row-wise Gaussian approximation over convex sets for the aligned difference, recovering prior rank-one results as special cases. For practical inference, we develop an online multiplier bootstrap algorithm and prove its consistency. Beyond streaming PCA, our techniques contribute to Gaussian approximation and bootstrap inference for nonconvex stochastic approximation.

</details>


### [78] [Sharper Regret Bounds for Time-Varying Gaussian Process Bandits with Constant Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18863)
*Matthias Mandl,Hanne Kekkonen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究时变环境下的贝叶斯优化，其中未知奖励函数根据高斯过程漂移模型演化。作者证明GP-UCB算法可以使用恒定探索参数运行，而非随时间范围增长，并通过每轮局部置信事件获得依赖漂移率的期望遗憾界。对于平方指数核，在持续漂移情况下获得了更优的时间变化最大信息增益界和期望平均遗憾界。仿真验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的GP-UCB在时变环境下的分析通常要求探索参数随时间范围增长以维持均匀置信界，这在实际应用中不理想且不灵活。作者希望证明在漂移模型下，使用恒定探索参数也能获得有效的遗憾保证，从而简化算法部署并提高实用性。

Method: 采用每轮局部置信事件（per-round local confidence events）取代均匀置信界，通过局部置信分析允许恒定探索参数。推导了更紧的时间变化最大信息增益界，并基于此获得期望遗憾界和实际（realized）遗憾界。对于平方指数核，分析了持续漂移机制下的具体渐近速率。

Result: 证明了GP-UCB在恒定探索参数下可获得依赖漂移率的期望遗憾界。对于平方指数核，得到γ̃_T/T = Õ(ε^{1/2})且期望平均遗憾为Õ(ε^{1/4})。同时获得了实际遗憾保证，仿真验证了探索参数对1/ε的对数依赖关系。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，通过局部置信事件分析，GP-UCB在时变环境下无需将探索参数随时间范围增长即可获得理论保证，减轻了算法对探索参数的敏感性，并为实践中的参数选择提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We study Bayesian optimization in a time-varying environment where the unknown reward function evolves according to a Gaussian process drift model. Existing GP-UCB analyses in this setting typically require the exploration parameter to grow with the horizon to maintain uniform confidence bounds. Using per-round local confidence events, we show that GP-UCB can instead be run with a constant exploration parameter and obtain an expected-regret bound whose coefficient depends on the drift rate. We also derive a sharper time-varying maximum-information-gain bound. For the squared exponential kernel, it yields $\tildeγ_T/T=\widetilde{\mathcal O}(ε^{1/2})$ and expected average regret $\widetilde{\mathcal O}(ε^{1/4})$ in the persistent-drift regime. The same constant-exploration analysis also yields realized-regret guarantees. Simulations support the predicted logarithmic dependence of the bound-suggested exploration parameter on $1/ε$.

</details>


### [79] [Diffusion Models for High-Dimensional Clustered Data: Intrinsic-Dimension Adaptivity via Bayesian Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19067)
*Yuga Iguchi,Paul Fearnhead*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究扩散模型对多模态高维数据的自适应性，其中数据由多个具有各自低维结构的簇构成。作者以K-混合高斯分布为框架，将去噪过程解释为动态贝叶斯分类器，并证明当信噪比达到Θ(log(KD)/D)时后验类别概率集中于单一簇；通过分别分析混合阶段和簇承诺阶段，证明KL误差界线性依赖于簇的最大内蕴维度（至多对数因子），即使K随D多项式增长也成立，优于环境维度界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型的理论研究存在两条平行线索：一是定量误差界，二是定性分析去噪阶段。本文旨在将两者统一，研究扩散模型对多模态高维数据（多簇、每簇具有低维结构、簇间距依赖维度D）的结构几何自适应性，以混合高斯分布为规范框架来刻画这种几何结构。

Method: 采用K-混合高斯分布作为基准框架。首先将混合分数解释为后验加权平均的簇级分数，证明后验类别概率在信噪比达到Θ(log(KD)/D)时集中到单一簇；其次将去噪过程分为混合阶段和簇承诺阶段分别分析，证明KL误差界线性依赖于最大簇内蕴维度（乘对数因子），适用于K随D多项式增长及异质近似低秩协方差的情形。

Result: 两个主要理论结果：(1) 去噪可作为动态贝叶斯分类器，后验类别概率以高概率集中在单一簇；(2) KL误差界仅依赖最大内蕴维度而非环境维度D，即使簇数K随D多项式增长也成立，扩展了现有低维自适应分析到多模态异构近似低秩协方差分布。

Conclusion: 本文弥合了扩散模型定量误差界与定性去噪阶段分析之间的鸿沟，证明了扩散模型能自适应地利用多模态数据的结构化几何（低维簇结构），实现维度无关的误差界，为理解扩散模型在真实复杂数据上的表现提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: The empirical success of diffusion models in generative modelling has motivated theoretical work, including quantitative error bounds and qualitative analyses that characterise the different phases of denoising. We bring these two areas together by studying the adaptivity of diffusion models to the structured geometry of multimodal high-dimensional data that consists of multiple clusters in $\mathbb{R}^D$, each with its own low-dimensional structure, and inter-cluster separation depending on $D$. We employ $K$-mixture Gaussian distributions as a canonical framework to capture this geometry and establish two theoretical results. First, we interpret denoising as a dynamical Bayesian classifier: the mixture score is a posterior-weighted average of cluster-wise scores, and we show that, with high probability, the posterior class probabilities concentrate on a single cluster once the signal-to-noise ratio reaches the scale $Θ(\log (KD)/D)$. Second, by separately analysing the denoising process in its mixing and cluster-commitment phases, we prove that the KL error bound depends linearly on the maximum intrinsic dimension of a cluster, up to a logarithmic factor, even when $K$ grows polynomially with $D$. This improves on ambient-dimensional bounds and extends existing low-dimensional adaptivity analyses to multimodal distributions with heterogeneous, approximately low-rank covariances.

</details>


### [80] [Learning Random Geometric Graphs Drawn in Probabilistic Metric Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19082)
*Dalia Chakrabarty,Kangrui Wang,Chuqiao Zhang,Ye Liu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的数据驱动学习方法，用于在概率度量空间中构建多元数据集的随机几何图（RGG）。作者引入了一个表示顶点连接度与变量相关性之间差异的随机变量，以其累积分布函数作为距离函数，构建了一种"软RGG"（Soft RGG），其中每条边以识别出的概率存在，并提出了基于拒绝采样的边概率学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机几何图学习方法在处理多元数据集时存在局限，难以适应不同类型的观测变量、概率分布以及任意大小的数据。需要一种通用且数据驱动的图学习方法，能够适用于任意类型的数据集。

Method: 1) 引入"差异变量"（disparity variable），表征图中两顶点连接度与该两顶点所关联随机变量之间的相关性差异；2) 以该差异变量的闭式累积分布函数（cdf）作为宿主空间的距离函数，若节点间距离低于选定截止概率则存在边；3) 形成软RGG（Soft RGG），每条存在的边均有识别出的概率；4) 提出基于拒绝采样（Rejection Sampling）的技术来学习任意边的概率；5) 推导顶点的期望度分布为局部的且依赖于观测间相关矩阵；6) 若相关矩阵未知，可利用所提出的闭式后验概率密度函数从数据中学习。

Result: 通过在高维多元真实数据集上学习多个RGG来验证所提出的图学习方法，展示了该方法的有效性和通用性。

Conclusion: 该方法能够在概率度量空间中有效学习多元数据集的软随机几何图，适用于任意类型和规模的观测数据，边的存在性和概率均可明确识别，且能通过数据驱动方式学习相关矩阵参数。

Abstract: We present a new data-driven learning of a Random Geometric Graph (RGG) of a multivariate dataset, where the graph is drawn in a probabilistic metric space. This graph learning works for generic datasets, irrespective of the type of the observables; their probability distributions; or size of the data. We identify a metric of the space that the graph is drawn in, as a probability distribution of a random variable that we introduce, namely, a variable that represents the disparity between the connectedness of two vertices of the graph, and the correlation between the two random variables that are attached to the respective vertex. It is the closed-form {\it{cdf}} of this disparity variable that we advance as the distance function of the host space of the learnt RGG, such that the edge exists between any two nodes, if this inter-nodal distance falls short of a chosen cutoff probability. Drawing the RGG in this probabilistic space leads to the graph being an Soft RGG, such that any edge - if it exists - exists with an identified probability. We forward a simple Rejection Sampling-based technique for learning the probability of any edge. The expected degree distribution of a vertex of this RGG is identified as local, and dependent on the inter-observable correlation matrix. If said correlation matrix is not known, it can be learnt given the data, using its closed-form posterior probability density function, that we forward. We illustrate our graph learning method by learning multiple RGGs of highly multivariate real datasets.

</details>

### [81] [Global Index on Responsible AI 2026 : Conceptual Framework and Methodology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18122)
*Fola Adeleke,Rachel Adams,Ayantola Alayande,Daniela Benavente,Ana Florido,Nicolás Grossman,Leah Junck*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文报告了《全球负责任AI指数》（GIRAI）第二版的方法论。该指数从三个维度重构为五个主题领域（包容与多样性、伦理与可持续、劳动与技能、信任与安全、AI在公共服务中的应用），共38个指标，分为AI政策（17项，权重60%）、民间社会组织参与（5项，权重10%）和赋能条件（15项，权重30%）三大支柱，另设政府使用不可接受风险AI（URAI）指标作为责任惩罚项。数据由135个国家的研究者通过结构化全球调查收集，辅以二手数据，最终将结果归一化至100分制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一版GIRAI在区分框架存在性与实施程度方面不够清晰，且维度结构不够精细。第二版旨在通过加强框架存在与实施之间的区别、重构维度结构、引入更细粒度的框架质量变量，并应用多阶段评审与验证流程，更准确地评估各国负责任AI治理的实际状况。

Method: GIRAI 2.0采用五维度框架（包容与多样性、伦理与可持续、劳动与技能、信任与安全、AI公共服务应用），下设38个指标，组织为三大支柱：AI政策（17项，主要数据）、CSO参与（5项，主要数据）和赋能条件（15项，二手数据）。数据通过135个国家的国家级研究者结构化全球调查收集，数据点的数量、范围、可执行性、主题覆盖和实施水平被编码为数值变量，归一化至100分制，按60%（AI政策）、10%（CSO参与）和30%（赋能条件）的支柱权重聚合。对存在URAI证据的国家施加扣分惩罚。此外进行了独立的统计预审计以评估框架的连贯性和稳健性。

Result: 该报告提供了一个系统化的跨国家比较方法，能够识别各国在负责任AI治理中承诺转化为可执行保护的进展以及关键差距所在。通过多阶段审查验证和独立统计预审计，确保了框架的连贯性和稳健性。

Conclusion: GIRAI 2.0通过更精细的指标体系和权重设计，为政策制定者、民间社会和AI开发者提供了系统的跨国家比较基准，有助于识别负责任AI治理实施中的关键差距与最佳实践。

Abstract: This report presents the methodology of the Global Index on Responsible AI (GIRAI), 2nd Edition. This edition refines the 1st Edition by strengthening the distinction between framework existence and implementation, restructuring dimensions from three to five thematic areas, introducing more granular variables for framework quality, and applying a multi-stage review and validation process. An independent statistical pre-audit was conducted to assess the coherence and robustness of the framework. GIRAI assesses responsible AI governance across five dimensions: Inclusion and Diversity, Ethics and Sustainability, Labour and Skills, Trust and Safety, and Use of AI in Public Service. Each dimension has a number of indicators (38 in total), organised into three pillars, namely AI Policy (17 indicators on government frameworks and implementation, assessed through primary data), CSO Engagement (5 indicators, primary data), and Enabling Conditions (15 indicators on the structural factors shaping responsible AI governance, assessed through secondary data), and a government Use of Unacceptable Risk AI (URAI) indicator (primary data), applied separately as an accountability penalty to the final score. Data was collected by 135 country-level researchers through a structured global survey, complemented by secondary datasets. The count, scope, enforceability, thematic coverage, and implementation levels of the data points are coded into numerical variables, normalised to a scale of 100, aggregated through pillar weights of 60% (AI policy), 10% (CSO Engagement), and 30% (Enabling conditions). A deduction penalty is applied for countries with evidence of URAI. This documentation enables systematic cross-national comparison, supporting policymakers, civil society, and AI developers to identify where commitments are translating into enforceable protections and where critical gaps remain.

</details>


### [82] [FairGlucose: A CGM Fairness Benchmark Reveals Subgroup Disparities Hidden in Population-Level Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18296)
*Junjie Luo,Xuzhe Zhi,Rui Han,Abhimanyu Kumbara,Anand K. Iyer,Mansur E. Shomali,Ritu Agarwal,Guodong Gordon Gao*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文构建了FairGlucose数据集（300名患者、12个人口统计学分层、132,480个预测样本），通过基准测试33个CGM血糖预测模型，发现总体外部验证指标稳定但亚组间存在显著差异（T1D比T2D预测误差高6 mg/dL），证明群体层面的验证不足以评估数字健康AI的公平性，呼吁将亚组分群报告作为默认标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于CGM的AI工具即将进入临床部署，但其在不同人口统计学群体间的准确性是否公平尚未得到充分测试，现有评估方法可能掩盖亚组间的不平等。

Method: 构建了FairGlucose数据集，包含300名患者、12个人口统计学分层（年龄×性别×糖尿病类型），132,480个预测样本和3,945个行为事件。对33个模型（涵盖4个模型家族）进行2小时血糖预测基准测试，并进行亚组层面的偏差分析。

Result: 整体OOD指标稳定（约1.0），但亚组比率范围从0.8到1.4；T1D患者比T2D患者的预测误差高6 mg/dL（p<0.001），且该差异在全部33个模型中持续存在。前沿LLM比专用神经模型差1-6 mg/dL；行为事件贡献可忽略（约0.1 mg/dL）。

Conclusion: 群体层面的外部验证不足以评估数字健康AI的公平性，亚组差异源于预测任务本身而非特定架构，应将亚组分群报告作为默认评估标准。

Abstract: As CGM-based AI tools approach clinical deployment, whether their accuracy is equitable across patient demographics remains insufficiently tested. To enable this evaluation, we constructed FairGlucose, a 300-patient CGM cohort balanced across 12 demographic strata (age x gender x type 1/type 2 diabetes), with 132,480 forecasting samples and 3,945 unique behavioral events (meals, exercise, medication) logged by 81 patients. Benchmarking 33 models across four families on 2-hour glucose forecasting, we find that population-level external validation can conceal substantial subgroup disparities. Aggregate out-of-distribution metrics appear stable (approximately 1.0), yet subgroup-level ratios range from 0.8 to 1.4, with T1D patients showing 6 mg/dL higher prediction error than T2D (p < 0.001). This disparity persists across all 33 models, suggesting a property of the prediction task rather than any single architecture. Further analysis shows that subgroup performance gaps align with the proportion of clinically hard cases, and that input-length sensitivity varies across demographics, motivating personalized configurations. Frontier LLMs underperform specialized neural models by 1-6 mg/dL; behavioral events contribute negligibly (approximately 0.1 mg/dL) even under oracle event access. These findings establish that population-level validation alone is insufficient for equity assessment of digital health AI, motivating subgroup-disaggregated reporting as a default standard.

</details>


### [83] [CentaurBench: Benchmarking LLM Capabilities on Augmenting vs. Automating Real-World Work Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18554)
*Pattaraphon Kenny Wongchamcharoen,Kris Gulati,Min Min Fong,Abhishek Nagaraj*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一框架，在七个经济相关的真实任务中评估LLM自动化任务与增强其他智能体性能的能力差异。研究发现自动化排名与增强排名仅存在中等程度的相关性，自动化胜者在五个任务上输掉了增强比赛，且辅助并不总是带来正向收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM基准主要以自动化工作任务的排名来衡量模型能力，但实际中模型常被用作辅助其他（人类或LLM）智能体的工具。因此模型选择的关键问题不仅是哪个模型产出最好，还包括哪个模型最能提升另一个较弱智能体的工作表现。自动化能力无法完全代表辅助质量。

Method: 设计统一评估框架，包含两种模式：自动化模式（助手直接产出交付物）和增强模式（助手为标准化低能力工作模型撰写辅助文本，由工作模型产出交付物）。在七个经济相关的真实世界任务上进行评估，使用LLM评审团结合任务特定评分标准进行盲法成对比较，每个任务重复十次运行。

Result: 两种模式的排名仅呈中等程度相关；自动化赢家在五个任务上输掉了增强比赛；辅助并非总是正向的——无辅助的工作模型在三个任务上排名优于所有辅助条件；平均而言只有一种模型的指导优于无指导。

Conclusion: 自动化能力无法作为辅助质量的完整代理指标，这激励未来的基准测试应根据模型在人类-AI和多智能体系统中扮演的角色来评估模型。

Abstract: Most LLM benchmarks rank models on their ability to automate work tasks. In practice, however, models are often used to assist other (human or LLM) agents. The question that drives model selection is therefore not only which model produces the best output, but which model most improves the work of another (weaker) agent. We introduce a unified framework that evaluates the capability of models to automate and augment another agent's performance. Across seven economically grounded real-world tasks, an assistant model writes assistance text for a standardized lower-capacity worker model, which produces the deliverable. In automation mode, the assistant produces the output directly. Outputs are scored through blind pairwise comparisons by an LLM judge panel with task-specific rubrics, replicated across ten runs. Rankings across the two regimes are only modestly correlated, and the automation winner loses augmentation on five of seven tasks. Assistance is not reliably positive. The unaided worker outranks every assisted condition on three tasks, and only one model's guidance beats no guidance on average. These results suggest that automation ability is an incomplete proxy for assistance quality, motivating benchmarks that evaluate models according to the roles they play in human-AI and multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [84] [Capability-Based Planning for AI Crisis Preparedness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18357)
*Isaak Mengesha,Charlie Collins,Juan Felipe Cerón Uribe,Salvatore d'Ambrosio,Vickie Ellis*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出将基于能力的规划（capability-based planning）方法应用于AI危机应对准备，替代传统"预测-行动"范式，通过情景库、能力评级和优先级决策三个步骤构建方法论框架，并在四类最严重的AI威胁类别上进行试点验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 国防和国土安全领域已广泛应用基于能力的规划，但AI领域尚未采用。政府目前的AI准备遵循"先预测后行动"范式，即按可能性和影响对风险排序，然后为预期危害最高的风险做准备。然而AI难以预测：专家对时间线的估计相差数量级，官方审查也承认基于可能性的风险评估恰恰在此类风险上失效。

Method: 借鉴深度不确定性下的决策原则，提出三部分方法论框架：(1) 情景库：在声明的轴线上系统采样生成情景；(2) 评级程序：基于粗略的分级标准，评估每项政府能力在每个情景下的表现；(3) 优先级步骤：将生成的矩阵映射到政府可能采纳的决策规则上，据此确定准备优先级。

Result: 通过对四类最严重的AI赋能威胁类别进行试点，展示了该工具能够产生的见解类型，并为基于能力的规划作为AI危机应对准备的实用工具提供了概念验证。

Conclusion: 基于能力的规划可以作为一种实用的AI危机应对准备工具，弥补当前预测范式在AI此类高度不确定性风险上的不足。

Abstract: Capability-based planning drives preparedness in defense and homeland security, but has yet to be applied seriously to AI. Government AI preparations follow a predict-then-act paradigm: rank risks by likelihood and impact, then prepare for the highest expected harm. AI resists prediction: expert timelines disagree by orders of magnitude, and official reviews concede that likelihood-based risk assessment fails for exactly this class of risk. Drawing on principles of decision making under deep uncertainty, we propose a methodological framework in three parts: a scenario library sampled systematically across declared axes; a rating procedure that assesses each government capability against each scenario on coarse, gated criteria; and a prioritization step that maps the resulting matrix onto decision rules a government might adopt. Through a pilot across the four most severe AI-enabled threat classes, we illustrate the kind of insight the instrument yields and provide a proof of concept for capability-based planning as a practical tool for AI crisis preparedness.

</details>


### [85] [The Fabricated Front: Generative AI and the Opacity of Workplace Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18369)
*Tom van Nuenen,Pratik S. Sachdeva,Sahiba Chopra*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文探讨生成式AI如何在工作场所中重构人际互动的"前台"（front），提出"努力不透明性"（effort opacity）的五种机制——声音、来源、脆弱性、注意力、投入——并指出工作者的实践围绕身份机制与劳动机制呈现不对称性，主张治理任务应从普遍披露转向"投入管理"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GenAI研究集中于生产率、岗位替代与偏见等产出导向议题，忽视了AI在工作场所产生的互动性重组。作者以"努力不透明性"概念为出发点，揭示GenAI使互动线索失去诊断性、削弱协作信任的问题，进而需要深入考察日常职场互动中不透明性得以产生的互动机制。

Method: 以Goffman的拟剧论（dramaturgical framework）为理论框架，分析Anthropic的AI Interviewer数据集中1,250份访谈转录文本，识别出五种不透明性机制：voice（措辞指向谁的立场）、provenance（谁能对作品负责）、vulnerability（工作者是否表现出不确定）、attention（工作者是否投入）、investment（产出反映多少劳动）。

Result: 发现专业人士会维护身份类机制（声音、来源、脆弱性），却自由地制造劳动类机制（注意力、投入）的不透明性。这种不对称根源于当代工作以产出为中心的组织方式——交付物已取代劳动过程成为衡量依据。

Conclusion: 治理任务应从"普遍披露"转向"投入管理"（involvement management），即明确哪些人类投入形式（注意力、努力、判断）必须保持可核查，以及向谁可核查。职场AI政策若坚持普遍披露原则，将系统性误认一个可核查性本就因受众而异的互动场域。

Abstract: Generative AI (GenAI) has become a fixture of workplace life. Current research asks chiefly what this implies for jobs and outputs, measured in productivity, displacement, or bias. What remains underexamined are the interactional reconfigurations that GenAI produces at work. The emerging concept of effort opacity has begun to fill this gap by highlighting the systematic decoupling of observable output from human engagement. When GenAI makes interactional cues less diagnostic, it weakens the reciprocal exchange that sustains collaborative trust. Extending this account of effort opacity, we examine the interactional mechanics that produce opacity in everyday workplace encounters. Drawing on Erving Goffman's dramaturgical framework and 1,250 interview transcripts from Anthropic's AI Interviewer dataset, we identify five opacity mechanisms through which workplace fronts are reorganized: voice (whose stance the words index), provenance (who can stand behind the artifact), vulnerability (whether the worker is uncertain), attention (whether the worker is engaged), and investment (how much labor the output reflects). We show that professionals defend the identity mechanisms while freely producing opacity around the labor mechanisms, and trace this asymmetry to the output-centered organization of contemporary work, where deliverables already stand in for the labor process that produced them. The governance task, accordingly, is one of involvement management: specifying which forms of human involvement (attention, effort, judgment) must remain inspectable, and to whom. Workplace AI policies built on universal disclosure will systematically misrecognize a social field in which inspectability is already audience-relative.

</details>


### [86] [Turning interest into institutional change: teaching advocacy for sustainable research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18601)
*Kirsty Pringle,Lorna Smith,Erinma Ochu,Greg Wilson*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个开放获取课程，旨在教授研究人员、研究软件工程师和研究技术专业人员如何将环保动机转化为持久的组织变革，以降低数字研究的环境影响。课程围绕UNICEF五步倡导循环构建，涵盖利益相关者分析、权力映射、联盟建设、叙事、框架与信息传递及评估，扎根于英国政策背景，采用虚构案例教学，并以CC BY 4.0许可免费开放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字研究领域的环境影响亟需系统性变革，但许多研究人员和技术专业人员虽有行动动机，却缺乏将动机转化为持久组织变革所需的技能。

Method: 开发一个以UNICEF五步倡导循环为框架的开放课程，涵盖利益相关者分析、权力映射、联盟建设、叙事传达和评估等核心模块；扎根于英国政策环境（如环境可持续性协议和UKRI战略）；采用虚构案例使概念具体化；设计为双层资源（工作坊幻灯片+扩展自学笔记）；在2026年6月的NetDRIVE夏季学校进行试点交付。

Result: 课程已成功开发并完成试点交付，以CC BY 4.0许可免费公开提供。具体成效数据在摘要中未详细报告。

Conclusion: 该课程填补了研究技术人员在倡导和组织变革技能方面的培训空白，通过免费开放获取的方式，为数字研究领域的可持续发展赋能提供了可复用的教育资源。

Abstract: Systemic change across the digital research landscape is required to reduce the environmental impact of digital research, but while many researchers and technical professionals are motivated to act, they often lack the skills required to translate motivation into lasting organisational change. We present an open-access course that teaches the foundations of advocacy and organisational change to researchers, research software engineers, and research technical professionals. Structured around the UNICEF five-step advocacy cycle, the course covers stakeholder analysis, power mapping, coalition building, storytelling, framing and messaging, and evaluation. It is grounded in the UK policy landscape, including the Concordat for Environmental Sustainability and the UKRI Environmental Sustainability Strategy, and uses a fictional case study to make concepts concrete. The course is designed as a dual-layer resource in which workshop slides and extended self-study notes are contained in a single source material. A short pilot was delivered at the NetDRIVE Net Zero DRI Summer School in June 2026. The course is freely available under a CC BY 4.0 licence.

</details>


### [87] [Epistemic Subordination: Generative AI and the Infrastructure of Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18758)
*Gilad Abiri,Emanuel V. Towfigh*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出"认识论从属"（epistemic subordination）这一概念：生成式AI不仅产生偏见输出，而是在训练阶段将主流文化的认知方式编码为知识本身的默认基础设施。少数群体的认识论被吸收但被结构性边缘化，这种统一伤害跨越反歧视法、文化与语言权利、民主观点多元主义三个法律领域，而现有法律均因只规范下游应用层面而无法解决这一问题。文章主张法律必须介入模型训练层面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对生成式AI偏见的讨论将偏见视为可审计、可修正的离散问题，但作者指出这种理解是错误的。偏见不是零散的现象，而是嵌入训练架构中的系统性认识论条件。同时，现有法律框架（反歧视法、文化语言权利、观点多元主义）虽然覆盖不同领域，却都只在输出和应用层面进行监管，无法触及伤害的真正源头——模型训练过程。

Method: 本文采用概念建构与跨领域法律分析的论证方法。首先提出"认识论从属"这一新概念，阐述其如何通过训练过程的概率压缩机制形成；然后将其作为统一的分析框架，逐一考察反歧视法、文化语言权利、民主观点多元主义三个法律领域为何及如何无法应对这一伤害；最后基于"救济必须匹配伤害发生地"的原则，论证法律监管应从下游决策层转向模型训练层。

Result: 文章论证了三个核心结论：第一，生成式AI的伤害本质是认识论层面的结构性从属而非离散的偏见；第二，三个法律领域因共享"下游监管"的结构性缺陷而均告失效；第三，法律救济的范式必须从规范"应用"转向治理"训练"，即对模型训练过程中认识论压缩机制本身的监管。

Conclusion: 生成式AI的认识论伤害是架构性的、统一的，不能通过下游审计和纠正来解决。法律必须在模型训练层面建立治理机制，将少数群体的认识论从属问题作为基础性的法律议题来对待，而非仅通过零散的反偏见规则修补。

Abstract: Generative AI does not merely produce biased outputs. It encodes the majority's way of knowing as the default infrastructure of knowledge itself. We call this epistemic subordination. The training process compresses the full breadth of human expression into a single probabilistic model whose statistical baseline reflects the languages, assumptions, and cultural frameworks of the dominant culture. Minority epistemologies are not excluded but absorbed: present in the training data, yet structurally subordinated in the output. The result is not a collection of discrete biases that can be audited and corrected. It is an epistemic condition embedded in the architecture from which all outputs emerge. This unified harm cuts across three legal domains -- anti-discrimination law, cultural and linguistic rights, and democratic viewpoint pluralism -- and each fails to address it for the same structural reason: existing law regulates downstream, at the level of decisions and applications. The remedy must match the site of harm. If epistemic subordination is produced at the level of model training, then law must learn to govern at that level.

</details>


### [88] [Open at the Edge, Captured at the Center: llama.cpp and the Political Economy of Local AI Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19001)
*Woohyeuk Lee,Hanlin Li,David Gray Widder*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文通过混合方法分析llama.cpp项目（涵盖7,681个合并的pull request及相关讨论），揭示本地推理基础设施虽然扩大了执行层面的参与，但将价值捕获转移到了基础设施层面——即硬件厂商、模型分发者和核心维护者手中，而非模型所有者和个人贡献者。作者主张开放性的维护需要超越模型发布条件，关注基础设施层的政策机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放AI学术研究聚焦于模型发布和云端生态系统，忽视了使开源权重模型能够在用户自有设备上运行的本地推理基础设施。作者希望通过填补这一研究空白，揭示本地推理中权力的重新分配与价值捕获的隐性机制。

Method: 采用混合方法分析：收集2023年3月至2026年3月间llama.cpp的7,681个合并pull request，并结合仓库讨论、企业声明和贡献者博客进行三角验证。具体关注三个维度：硬件后端、模型集成劳动，以及Hugging Face在2026年2月对该项目的吸收。

Result: 研究发现：本地推理虽然拓宽了执行层面的技术参与，但控制权却向硬件供应商、模型分发机构和核心维护者集中；模型所有者与个人贡献者承担了让模型可运行的成本。这些动态表明，云端之外维持开放性需要关注使模型可运行的基础设施。

Conclusion: 作者呼吁政策机制应超越模型发布条件，延伸至基础设施层——包括分析格式依赖与供应商影响、制定模型兼容性要求，以及为推理工具提供持续公共资金支持。

Abstract: Open AI scholarship has focused on model releases and cloud ecosystems, leaving the local inference infrastructure that makes open-weight models runnable on user-owned devices largely unexamined. We address this gap through a mixed-methods analysis of llama.cpp, combining 7,681 merged pull requests from March 2023 through March 2026 with repository discussions, corporate statements, and contributor blogs. We show that local inference broadens participation at execution while relocating capture into the infrastructure that makes execution possible. Through hardware backends, model integration labor, and Hugging Face's February 2026 absorption of the project, we document how control shifts to hardware vendors, model distributors, and core maintainers while model owners and individual contributors bear the cost of making models runnable. These dynamics suggest that preserving openness outside the cloud requires attention to the infrastructure that makes models runnable, not just to the models themselves. This calls for policy mechanisms---analysis of format dependencies and vendor influence, model compatibility requirements, and sustained public funding for inference tooling---that extend beyond model release conditions to the infrastructure layer.

</details>


### [89] [Hot Games: Towards a Holistic Assessment of the Planet Warming Emissions of Video Games based on 2024-2025 Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19040)
*Mike Hazas,Kevin C. Dalli,Arjun Menon,Ben Abraham,Ossian Nordgren*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文对视频游戏的生产和使用的全球环境影响进行了全面评估，整合了游戏开发、硬件、销售、下载量、游戏时间以及多人和云游戏服务订阅等公开数据，更新了2020和2022年的数据，并提供了可被未来批评和改进的详细排放核算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前虽有针对特定平台或公司的研究报告，但缺乏对视频游戏行业全球影响的综合评估。作者旨在填补这一空白，提供跨类别的广泛环境（特别是碳排放）影响分析。

Method: 汇集多种公开数据源，包括游戏开发、硬件制造、游戏销量、下载大小、各平台游戏时间以及多人和云游戏订阅服务等数据，对视频游戏全生命周期相关的排放进行核算，并以2020和2022年的数据作为基线进行更新和对比。

Result: 提供了一个涵盖广泛类别的全球视频游戏产业排放核算，并确保核算框架足够详细，以便未来被学术界或产业界审视、批评和改进。

Conclusion: 视频游戏产业的环境影响已不容忽视，本研究的核算框架为后续更精细、更可验证的全球评估奠定了基础，同时也为产业减排和政策制定提供了参考依据。

Abstract: Following on recent reports on specific platforms or companies, this paper provides an assessment of the global impact of the production and use of video games. It draws together publicly available data on game development, hardware, games sold, download sizes, time spent playing games on different platforms, and subscriptions to multiplayer and cloud game services. It provides an update to figures published 2020 and 2022. Crucially, our account of emissions related to video games considers a wide range of categories, yet contains enough detail to be critiqued and improved in the future.

</details>


### [90] [LearnAI: Just-in-Time AI Co-Creation Across Disciplines at a University](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19164)
*Weihao Qu,Ling Zheng,Chris Buzaid,Daniel Crawford*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文介绍了LearnAI框架，一个两层的即时AI共同创作模型，在综合性教学大学试点。广泛接触层通过在18门课程中嵌入短讲座来建立AI意识；定制共创层通过5阶段教学脚本提供一对一辅导。两个学期中，35位客户共同创作了36个作品集网站和20多个部署的Web应用。访谈表明客户对AI的看法从被动工具转变为协作工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI正在重塑专业和教育实践，但机构应对方式两极分化：要么面向大众的概念性工作坊，要么面向计算机专业学生的技术课程。缺乏适合不同能力水平学习者（从非编码者到高级学生）的中间地带，让他们能够按自己的经验水平参与常见AI任务。

Method: 提出LearnAI框架，包含两层：(1)广泛接触层——在现有课程中嵌入短演示以建立AI意识，覆盖18门课程5个学科；(2)定制共创层——提供可选的1对1辅导，使用5阶段教学脚本（问题框架、工具-任务映射、迭代共同提示、部署与验证、伦理反思），由训练的本科生导师执行。

Result: 两个学期中35位客户共同创作了36个作品集网站和20多个Web应用。对5位客户和2位导师的访谈显示客户对AI使用的描述发生转变，从被动应答机器转为受人类指导的协作工具。小型配对前后AI准备度数据(N=7)提供了初步描述性背景，导师记录展示了教学脚本的实施与调整。还报告了边界案例，包括感到不堪重负的客户和故意拒绝AI使用的受访者。

Conclusion: LearnAI框架是一个实用、可采纳的模型，在单一机构提供了初步证据。它为混合能力学习者提供了参与AI支持问题解决的中间空间，填补了概念工作坊与技术课程之间的空白，但需要在更多机构验证其可推广性。

Abstract: As generative AI reshapes professional and educational practice, institutions face a challenge: how to support diverse learners, from non-coders to advanced students, in building confidence and practice with AI-supported problem solving. Most institutional responses bifurcate into conceptual workshops for general audiences or technical courses for computer science majors, leaving few spaces where mixed-ability learners can engage common AI tasks at levels matched to their prior experience.
  This experience report presents the LearnAI Framework, a two-layer model for just-in-time AI co-creation piloted at a comprehensive teaching university. The Wide-Exposure Layer embeds short presentations in existing courses to build AI awareness at scale, reaching students and faculty across 18 courses in five disciplines. The Customized Co-Creation Layer provides opt-in, one-on-one sessions where clients work with trained undergraduate tutors through a 5-Stage Pedagogical Script: Problem Framing, Tool-Task Mapping, Iterative Co-Prompting, Deployment and Verification, and Ethical Reflection. Over two semesters, 35 clients co-created 36 portfolio websites and over 20 deployed web applications. Interviews with five clients and two tutors suggest a recurring change in how clients described AI use, shifting from treating AI as a passive answer machine to engaging it as a collaborative tool under human direction. A small paired pre/post AI readiness dataset (N = 7) provides preliminary descriptive context, and tutor accounts document how the pedagogical script was enacted and adapted across client types. We report on boundary cases including clients who felt overwhelmed and respondents who deliberately rejected AI use. This paper contributes a practical, adoptable framework with initial evidence from a single institution.

</details>


### [91] [Qualified Cross-References as a Verification Method: The Normative Environment of the EU AI Act](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19194)
*Nicola Fabiano*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种条款级别的限定性交叉引用模型和构建协议，用于法律知识库中交叉引用的描述。通过围绕《欧盟人工智能法案》（Regulation (EU) 2024/1689）的双语语料库，模型区分了五种交互类型，并引入"双向反转"（bidirectional inversion）作为验证操作，在构建过程中发现了六处错误条款引用、三处错误法律限定和一处已发表描述不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律交叉引用在策展知识库中不仅是链接的存在问题，还必须说明引用的法律性质、支撑条款、适用条件，并保证从两个法律文书出发的一致性。现有做法缺乏一个可检验、可验证的条款级模型来系统化描述这些交叉引用。

Method: 基于十四份围绕AI法案的双语文书构建条款级交叉引用模型。模型区分五种交互类型：直接文本引用、有界推定合规、无文本引用的实质性交互、中介交叉、制度类比；并将应用性交互与定义性重叠作为独立维度处理。核心方法是"双向反转"：从A法案向B法案记录的关系，需从B视角结合双方条款重建，作为验证操作而非重复表格。

Result: 在构建过程中，该协议发现六处错误条款引用、三处不准确的法律限定，以及两处已发表描述中同一交互的差异。语料库还表明限定很重要：对EU 2019/881号法规的引用承载了AI法案对有界网络安全推定合规的推定，而相关产品立法通过法律上不同的机制使用相同的认证框架。

Conclusion: 贡献是一个监管环境的法律交互图谱，以及一种可复现的方法，使策展交叉引用知识库变得可检查且内部可测试。

Abstract: Legal cross-references are commonly represented as links between instruments or provisions. For a curated legal knowledge base, the existence of a link is only the beginning of the claim: it must also state the legal character of the interaction, identify the provisions supporting it, preserve its conditions, and remain consistent when reached from either instrument. This paper presents a provision-level model and a construction protocol for qualified cross-references, developed through a bilingual corpus of fourteen instruments surrounding Regulation (EU) 2024/1689 (the AI Act). The model distinguishes direct textual reference, bounded presumption of conformity, substantive interaction without textual reference, mediated intersection, and institutional analogy, and treats applicative interaction and definitional overlap as independent dimensions. The methodological contribution is bidirectional inversion: a relationship documented from act A towards act B is reconstructed from B's perspective against the provisions of both. Inversion is not a duplicate table but a verification operation that tests provisions, qualification, direction, and conditions before deciding how the relationship should be rendered from either side. Applied during construction, the protocol surfaced six incorrect article references, three inaccurate legal qualifications, and one divergence between two published descriptions of the same interaction. The corpus also shows why qualification matters: one reference to Regulation (EU) 2019/881 carries the AI Act's bounded cybersecurity presumption for high-risk systems, while related product legislation uses the same certification framework through legally distinct mechanisms. The contribution is thus a map of one regulatory environment and a reproducible method for making curated cross-reference knowledge bases inspectable and internally testable.

</details>

### [92] [Real-Time Design of Public Transport Lines: Reconciling Adaptivity and Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18314)
*Duo Wang,Andrea Araldo,Mounim El-Yacoubi*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种动态设计结构化公交线路网络的管理策略，通过滚动时域优化方法在效率和适应性之间取得平衡。实验表明，与基于动态车辆路径问题（DVRP）的按需交通（DRT）路由相比，服务请求比例几乎翻倍；与传统公共交通（CPT）相比，服务更多请求且用户出行时间更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 按需交通（DRT）通过解决动态车辆路径问题（DVRPs）来路由，仅针对单个车辆轨迹进行局部调整，限制了需求整合的效率；而传统公共交通（CPT）虽然基于线路网络具有高需求整合能力，但其网络离线构建，无法适应实时需求变化。现有方法无法同时兼顾效率与适应性，因此需要一个既能动态适应需求又能保持高需求整合的策略。

Method: 提出一种公共交通管理策略，通过滚动时域（receding-horizon）优化方法动态设计结构化的线路网络。该方法在每次优化窗口内重新构建线路网络，使网络结构能够随需求变化实时调整，同时保持线路网络带来的需求整合优势。

Result: 使用真实世界出行请求数据验证，所提策略相比DVRP路由将服务请求比例几乎翻倍，同时相比CPT服务了更多请求，并实现了更低的用户出行时间。

Conclusion: 动态设计的结构化线路网络能够同时实现高需求整合效率和实时需求适应性，为公共交通管理提供了一种兼顾两者优势的可行方案。

Abstract: Demand-responsive transport (DRT) is typically routed by solving Dynamic Vehicle Routing Problems (DVRPs), where individual vehicle trajectories are adjusted on incoming requests. This limits demand consolidation and thus efficiency. On the other hand, Conventional Public Transport (CPT) bus systems are based on a network of lines and users find their routes on it, which provides high demand consolidation. However, such a network is built offline and cannot adapt to the demand. We propose a public transport management strategy that reconciles efficiency and adaptivity by dynamically designing a structured network of lines via a receding-horizon optimization approach. Using real-world trip requests, we show that we nearly double the fraction of served requests compared to DVRP-based routing, and we serve more requests than CPT with lower user trip times.

</details>


### [93] [A Benchmark Graph Dataset for Transient Stability Assessment of the IEEE 9-Bus System: 20,000 Scenarios with Full Generator Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18318)
*Hussein Suprême,Martin de Montigny,Arnaud Zinflou*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文发布了一个包含20,000个三相接地故障场景的暂态稳定评估基准数据集，基于IEEE 9节点系统构建。每个场景结合了交流潮流工作点与详细的电磁暂态仿真，提供了属性图、转子角与速度轨迹、机器参数、故障描述及稳定性标签，支持多种机器学习任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暂态稳定评估对电力系统安全至关重要，机器学习代理模型可加速评估，但缺乏结合动态真值、网络图结构和机器参数的开源数据集，严重制约了该领域的研究进展。

Method: 在IEEE 9节点系统上生成20,000个三相接地故障场景，每个场景将交流潮流工作点与电磁暂态仿真后故障响应耦合。数据集以属性图形式（9个母线、18条有向支路、10个节点特征、12个边特征）提供网络信息，包含3台发电机的转子角和速度完整轨迹、静态机器常数、故障描述以及基于惯量中心坐标的二元稳定性标签。通过18个故障位置和广泛的负荷/发电缩放生成近平衡分布，并使用固定随机种子和公开代码保证确定性和可复现性。

Result: 生成了近平衡的数据分布（48.96%稳定，51.04%不稳定），覆盖18个故障位置和广泛的负荷/发电缩放场景。数据集通过固定种子和公开代码实现完全确定性生成，已发布在IEEE DataPort上并具有持久DOI标识。

Conclusion: 该数据集填补了暂态稳定评估机器学习研究的公开数据空白，可支持稳定性分类、轨迹预测、稳定裕度和临界切除时间估计，以及拓扑感知、基于物理和混合学习方法之间的对比研究。

Abstract: Transient stability assessment determines whether a power system retains synchronism after a large disturbance. Machine-learning surrogates can accelerate it, but progress is limited by the lack of open datasets that combine dynamic ground truth, network graph structure, and machine parameters. We release a benchmark of 20,000 three-phase-to-ground fault scenarios on the IEEE 9-bus system. Each scenario couples an AC power-flow operating point with a detailed electromagnetic-transient simulation of the post-fault response. Every record provides the network as an attributed graph (nine buses, eighteen directed branches, ten node and twelve edge features), the full rotor-angle and speed trajectories of the three generators, the static machine constants, the fault description, and a center-of-inertia binary stability label. Wide load and generation scalings across eighteen fault locations yield a near-balanced distribution (48.96\% stable, 51.04\% unstable). Generation is deterministic and fully reproducible through fixed seeds and public code. The dataset is distributed on IEEE DataPort under a persistent DOI and supports stability classification, trajectory prediction, margin and critical-clearing-time estimation, and the comparison of topology-aware, physics-based, and hybrid learning methods.

</details>


### [94] [Model Predictive Supervisory Control for Hierarchical and Distributed UAS Traffic Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18353)
*Matheus P. Loures,Guilherme V. Raffo,Patrícia N. Pena*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于共享资源多智能体系统的分层模型预测监督控制（MPSC）框架，将滚动时域成本最优控制与基于监督控制理论（SCT）的监督相结合，以保障安全性、无阻塞性和资源排他性。该框架通过分层和可扩展的监督器及自动机模板实现可扩展性，支持分布式执行而无需整体综合。基于此框架构建了城市无人机交通管理（UTM）模型，支持时变需求下的取送任务高效执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统在共享资源下面临安全性、无阻塞性和资源排他性等挑战，同时需要成本最优控制。传统方法中，最优控制与安全监督往往分离或需要整体综合，导致可扩展性差，难以应用于大规模系统如城市无人机交通管理。

Method: 提出分层模型预测监督控制（MPSC）框架，将滚动时域（receding-horizon）成本最优控制与监督控制理论（SCT）相结合。采用分层设计与可扩展的监督器及自动机模板，实现分布式执行，避免整体综合（monolithic synthesis）带来的计算瓶颈。

Result: 基于该框架开发了城市无人机系统交通管理（UTM）模型，能够高效支持时变需求下的取送（pickup-and-delivery）任务。

Conclusion: MPSC框架通过分层结构与模板化设计有效兼顾了最优控制性能与安全约束，为大规模多智能体共享资源系统提供了可扩展的解决方案，并在UTM场景中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: This work proposes a hierarchical Model Predictive Supervisory Control (MPSC) framework for multi-agent systems with shared resources. MPSC integrates receding-horizon cost-optimal control with Supervisory control theory (SCT) based supervision that enforces safety, nonblockingness, and resource exclusivity. Scalability arises from hierarchical and scalable supervisor and automaton templates, enabling distributed execution without monolithic synthesis. Using this framework, this work develops an urban Unmanned aircraft system Traffic Management (UTM) model. The model supports pickup-and-delivery missions under time-varying demand efficiently.

</details>


### [95] [LiDAR-Derived Surface Priors for Multimodal Sensing-Assisted NLoS Beam Search in Indoor 60-GHz Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18396)
*Amod Ashtekar,Dalton Davis,Rafaela Lomboy,Omar Ibrahim,Raj Sai Sohel Bandari,Mohammed E. Eltayeb*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究利用LiDAR（激光雷达）作为先验信息以减少60-GHz mmWave IoT链路中非视距（NLoS）波束搜索开销。实验表明，LiDAR衍生的局部三维平面性描述符可在74.5%的测量位置将波束保持在穷举搜索3 dB范围内，同时将RF波束对探测减少72%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高度定向的60-GHz IoT链路可利用室内自然表面维持NLoS连接，但识别可行的NLoS路径需要大量的RF波束训练开销。作者希望探究LiDAR能否通过提供表面感知先验来减少搜索开销，且不假设光反射与mmWave反射之间存在直接映射关系。

Method: 提出一个框架，使用LiDAR衍生的几何和回波统计信息对候选传播方向进行排序，RF测量仍负责最终的波束选择。实验验证分三个阶段：(1) 受控LiDAR测量量化表面描述符随采集几何的变化；(2) L形走廊中的匹配LiDAR和60-GHz测量，检验描述符与表面介导RF响应的关联；(3) 房间级实验，使用穷举TX-RX波束图评估先验，不预先指定传播机制。

Result: 测量显示描述符可靠性取决于采集几何和点云表示；LiDAR与RF表面响应存在跨模态关联但不支持确定性RF功率预测。在房间实验中，局部三环3D平面性描述符在74.5%的测试位置将波束保持在穷举搜索3 dB内，同时相对于穷举探测在候选搜索区域减少72%的RF波束对探测。

Conclusion: LiDAR衍生的局部表面结构可作为面向通信的先验，用于集中RF探测并减少mmWave波束搜索的不确定性，为IoT场景下的高效NLoS连接提供可行方案。

Abstract: Highly directional 60-GHz Internet-of-Things (IoT) links can exploit naturally occurring indoor surfaces to sustain connectivity under blockage. Identifying viable non-line-of-sight (NLoS) paths, however, can require extensive RF beam training. This paper investigates whether LiDAR can reduce this search overhead by providing a surface-aware prior without assuming a direct mapping between optical return and mmWave reflection. The proposed framework uses LiDAR-derived geometry and return statistics to rank candidate propagation directions, while RF measurements remain responsible for final beam selection. The experimental validation is organized in three stages to separate descriptor robustness, cross-modal association, and beam-search performance. Controlled LiDAR measurements first quantify how geometric and radiometric surface descriptors vary with acquisition geometry. Matched LiDAR and 60-GHz measurements in an L-shaped corridor then determine whether these descriptors are associated with the measured surface-mediated RF response under a prescribed NLoS interaction. Finally, a separate room-scale campaign evaluates the resulting prior using exhaustive TX-RX beam maps without prescribing the underlying propagation mechanism. The measurements show that descriptor reliability depends on acquisition geometry and point-cloud representation, and that LiDAR and RF surface responses exhibit cross-modal association without supporting deterministic RF-power prediction. In the room experiment, local three-ring 3-D planarity retains a beam within 3 dB of exhaustive search at 74.5% of the measured locations while reducing RF beam-pair probing by 72% relative to exhaustive probing over the candidate search region. These results establish LiDAR-derived local surface structure as a communication-oriented prior for concentrating RF probing and reducing mmWave beam-search uncertainty.

</details>


### [96] [Power Estimation and Optimal Work-Charging Scheduling of Construction Electric Vehicles via Mobile Charging Stations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18494)
*Avik Ghosh,Akın Taşcıkaraoğlu,Daniela Rojas,Muhammed A. Beyazıt,Mohammad Reza Salehizadeh,Keaton Chia,Sasha Doppelt,Michael Ferry,Jan Kleissl,Sujit Dey,Yuanyuan Shi*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个场数据驱动的框架，将建筑电动车辆（CEV）的功耗估计与移动充电感知的工作调度相结合。在UCSD的真实建筑示范中，通过约束非负最小二乘法从电池SOC遥测数据中恢复每个子活动的平均功耗（预测误差17% NMAE）；随后构建混合整数规划模型，联合优化CEV工作/充电调度与移动充电站（MCS）的位置和充放电安排，在现实场景中实现比最佳基线低7%-96%的运营成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑电动车辆（CEV）作为柴油设备的清洁替代方案前景广阔，但其推广受限于现场充电基础设施稀疏、CEV移动性受限以及对功耗缺乏了解等障碍。

Method: 1) 功耗估计：利用UCSD真实建筑示范数据，将人工标注视频与电池SOC遥测同步，采用约束非负最小二乘恢复各子活动的平均功耗，并公开发布数据集。2) 联合调度：基于子活动功耗估计，构建混合整数规划，联合优化CEV工作与充电计划、MCS的位置/充电/放电，同时考虑能源费用、需求费用、碳排放、未完成任务惩罚、MCS移动以及CEV和MCS的物理与运营约束。

Result: 功耗模型在留出测试集上实现17%的归一化平均绝对误差（NMAE）；联合优化在所有现实场景中取得最低运营成本，比最佳基线低7%-96%，且大多数实例在一小时内求解到证明最优。

Conclusion: 该框架填补了CEV功耗估计缺失与移动充电调度不足的双重空白，为建筑电动化的实际部署提供了数据驱动且可求解的优化方案，相关数据集与代码已公开以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Construction electric vehicles (CEVs) are a promising clean alternative to diesel-powered construction equipment, but their adoption is constrained by sparse onsite charging infrastructure, limited CEV mobility, and insufficient understanding of their power consumption. We address these gaps through a field-data-driven framework coupling CEV power estimation with mobile-charging-aware work scheduling. First, using a real-world construction demonstration at the University of California, San Diego, we develop and validate a per-subactivity power estimation model for a compact electric excavator. Manually labeled video is synchronized with coarse battery state-of-charge (SOC) telematics, and constrained nonnegative least squares is used to recover each subactivity's average power consumption. The model predicts held-out test data within $17\%$ normalized mean absolute error (NMAE), and the accompanying dataset is released publicly. Second, leveraging the subactivity power estimates, we formulate a mixed-integer program that jointly optimizes CEV work and charging schedules together with the location, timing, and charging/discharging of mobile charging stations (MCSs) serving the CEVs. The optimization accounts for energy and demand charges, carbon emissions, unmet work penalties, MCS travel, and the physical and operational constraints of the CEVs and MCSs. Across realistic scenarios drawn from the demonstration, the proposed co-optimization attains the lowest operating cost in every case, being $7$--$96\%$ below the best-performing baseline, while solving most instances to proven optimality within an hour. Dataset and scripts are available at https://github.com/ghosh-avik/CEV-MCS-Power-Estimation-and-Joint-Scheduling.

</details>


### [97] [Toward Controllability-Aware Performance Measures: A Case Study on Controllable Highway Congestion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18510)
*Shreyaa Raghavan,Edgar Ramirez-Sanchez,Zhengbing He,Cathy Wu*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出"可控拥塞"(controllable congestion)这一新指标，用于量化智能交通系统(ITS)通过控制限速所能实现的最大拥塞改善上限，帮助交通管理机构评估ITS基础设施的部署潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前缺乏标准化指标来衡量ITS干预措施的改善潜力，导致交通管理机构可能在最不具潜力的地方部署基础设施，或投资于无法显著提升性能的基础设施。

Method: 基于METANET宏观交通模型的重新公式化，构建非线性优化框架，利用模型预测控制(MPC)求解，以量化系统延误的最大可减少量（即可控拥塞）。

Result: 通过合成场景和田纳西州I-24 SMART走廊的真实数据验证，发现可控拥塞与总延误基本独立——相同行程时间的两天，可控拥塞可在13%到80%之间变化。最小限速限制影响甚微，而限速更新频率和相邻龙门架的最大速度差影响显著。

Conclusion: 该框架使高速公路运营商能够区分结构性不可避免的拥塞与对ITS高度敏感的拥塞，从而支持更具成本效益的基于控制的交通基础设施部署。

Abstract: Advancements in emerging intelligent transportation systems (ITS) have shown immense benefit in reducing congestion, emissions, and accidents and enable lower-cost alternatives to highway lane expansion. However, there currently exists no standard metric by which agencies can assess the improvement potential from these interventions. As a result, they may fail to deploy infrastructure where it will be most promising or risk investing in infrastructure that does not meaningfully enhance performance. Our objective is to guide ITS deployment by developing a metric that quantifies the upper bound of congestion improvement from controlling speed limits on a highway. We propose controllable congestion, a metric that quantifies the maximum achievable reduction in system delay. To estimate controllable congestion, we develop a nonlinear optimization framework grounded in a reformulation of the METANET macroscopic traffic model and solved using model predictive control (MPC). Using both a synthetic scenario and highway data from the I-24 SMART Corridor in Tennessee, we find that controllable congestion is largely independent of total delay, revealing that for two days with identical travel times, controllable congestion can vary from 13\% to 80\%. We further show that the realizable share of this upper bound depends on the operational constraints imposed. On I-24, the minimum posted speed limit has little effect on controllable congestion, while frequency of speed limit update and maximum difference between adjacent gantries have large impacts. This framework allows highway operators to distinguish between congestion that is structurally unavoidable and congestion that is highly responsive to ITS, enabling more cost-effective deployment of control-based traffic infrastructure.

</details>


### [98] [Exact Robust Instability Analysis for Networked Dynamical Systems with Biological Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18553)
*Shinji Hara,Yutaka Hori,Tetsuya Iwasaki,Chung-Yao Kao,Sei Zhen Khong*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了名义上不稳定的不确定网络化动力系统中的鲁棒不稳定性问题，其中所有名义智能体共享相同的SISO LTI系统，且每个智能体受独立扰动影响。作者识别了三类网络结构（包括循环网络和特定秩亏网络），在这些结构下鲁棒不稳定性问题可简化为单个代表性SISO系统的分析，并推导了精确刻画这些网络类鲁棒不稳定半径的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究源于非线性动力学中维持周期性振荡的问题，精确分析通常难以处理。通过研究名义不稳定的线性网络系统在独立扰动下的鲁棒不稳定性，可以为这类周期振荡现象提供可处理的数学分析框架。

Method: 将鲁棒不稳定性问题简化为对单个代表性SISO系统的分析，识别了三类特定的网络结构（包括循环网络和秩亏网络），并推导了精确表征这些网络类鲁棒不稳定半径的充分条件。

Result: 针对所识别的三类网络结构，推导了精确刻画鲁棒不稳定半径的充分条件，并通过基因调控网络的振荡行为分析验证了所提方法的实用价值。

Conclusion: 提出的简化方法能够将复杂的网络化鲁棒不稳定性问题降维为单个SISO系统分析，为非线性系统中的周期振荡维持问题提供了理论工具，并在基因调控网络中展示了实际应用潜力。

Abstract: This paper investigates robust instability in nominally unstable uncertain networked dynamical systems, where all nominal agents share an identical single-input-single-output (SISO) linear time-invariant (LTI) system and each agent is subject to independent perturbations. This setting is motivated by the problem of sustaining periodic oscillations in nonlinear dynamics, for which exact analysis is generally intractable. We identify three classes of network structures including cyclic and certain rank-deficient networks for which the robust instability problem can be reduced to the analysis of a single representative SISO system. We derive sufficient conditions that exactly characterize the robust instability radius for these network classes. Finally, we demonstrate the practical utility of the proposed results by analyzing oscillatory behavior in a genetic regulatory network.

</details>


### [99] [Robust Instability Radius for Networked Dynamical Systems: Upper and Lower Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18561)
*Shinji Hara,Yutaka Hori,Tetsuya Iwasaki,Chung-Yao Kao,Sei Zhen Khong*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究不确定网络系统的鲁棒不稳定性问题，定义了鲁棒不稳定性半径（RIR），即使不稳定标称网络变稳定的最小稳定不确定性范数，并给出了RIR的上下界及精确刻画条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注网络系统的鲁棒稳定性，但对鲁棒不稳定性（即系统在何种不确定性下会失去不稳定性）缺乏系统刻画。理解鲁棒不稳定性对于安全关键系统设计和网络脆弱性分析具有重要意义。

Method: 将多智能体系统建模为对角不确定性、标称相同智能体与静态互联矩阵的反馈互联结构。利用小增益论证，推导网络稳定性的条件，并基于此建立RIR的上下界。针对秩为一的连通矩阵且对角元同号或为零的特殊情况，给出RIR的精确表达式。

Result: 推导出网络稳定性的判定条件，建立了RIR的上下界；在连通矩阵秩为一且对角元符号一致或为零的条件下，证明了RIR可由小增益论证精确刻画。

Conclusion: 本文为不确定网络系统的鲁棒不稳定性分析提供了理论框架，并在特定网络拓扑下给出了RIR的精确解，为评估网络系统在不稳定状态下的鲁棒性提供了量化工具。

Abstract: This paper is concerned with robust instability of uncertain network systems. We consider the multi-agent system described as a network of single-input-single-output agents with identical nominal dynamics subject to heterogeneous perturbations. The network description is formalized as a feedback interconnection of a diagonal uncertainty, nominal identical agents, and a static interconnection matrix. Assuming that the nominal network is unstable, we seek the robust instability radius (RIR), defined as the smallest norm of the stable uncertainty that renders the network stable. Conditions for the network stability are developed, and upper and lower bounds on the RIR are derived. When the network connectivity matrix is rank one and all diagonal entries share the same sign or are zero, we give conditions under which the RIR is exactly characterized by a small gain argument.

</details>


### [100] [Toward S^2C^2I-Integrated High-Altitude Platforms: Architectures, Cross-Functional Design, Evaluation, and Deployment Perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18587)
*Haoxiang Luo,Mohamed-Slim Alouini*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文对高空平台（HAPs）作为空天地一体化网络（SAGINs）中间层基础设施的传感、存储、通信、计算与智能（S²C²I）一体化整合进行了全面综述，提出了统一的HAP中心视角，并给出了从平台设计到网络部署的完整路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HAPs正从单纯的通信中继角色演变为同时提供感知、存储、通信、计算与智能服务的多维基础设施，但目前缺乏一个统一、系统化的综述来整合这些功能，亟需一个HAP中心的S²C²I一体化视角来指导该领域的发展。

Method: 本文采用系统综述方法：（1）回顾HAP基础、平台分类及其在SAGINs中的六大角色；（2）构建涵盖多平面连接、载荷功能拆分和云-边缘-HAP空间连续体的集成架构；（3）系统考察使能技术（异构射频/毫米波/太赫兹/自由空间光/混合链路、集成感知与通信、机载计算、存储缓存、AI编排）；（4）综合标准化进展、开源软件、测试平台和四级评估方法论，并通过应急响应案例研究验证。

Result: 应急响应案例研究表明，联合S²C²I编排能显著提升联合服务可用性，同时减少馈电链路流量。文章还汇总了标准化进展、现场证据以及从组件验证到任务级效能评估的四级评价体系。

Conclusion: 本文指出了未来研究方向（智能体AI、可信自主、目标导向语义操作、数字孪生、可持续/可认证/开源的HAP原生系统），并提供了从平台设计到网络级部署的连贯路线图，为SAGINs中HAP的S²C²I一体化发展奠定了系统性框架。

Abstract: High-altitude platforms (HAPs) are emerging as persistent middle-layer infrastructures for space-air-ground integrated networks (SAGINs), offering a favorable compromise among coverage, latency, endurance, and deployment flexibility. Their role, however, is evolving beyond communication relaying toward the joint provision of sensing, storage, communication, computing, and intelligence (S^2C^2I). This survey presents a unified HAP-centric perspective on S^2C^2I integration. We first review HAP fundamentals, platform categories, and their principal roles in SAGINs, including wide-area access, relaying, backhaul, edge service, low-altitude aerial coordination, and cross-layer orchestration. We then develop an integrated architecture spanning multi-plane connectivity, payload functional splits, and a cloud-edge-HAP space continuum with hierarchical data, control, computing, and storage loops. The enabling technologies are systematically examined, covering heterogeneous RF, millimeter-wave, terahertz, free-space optical, and hybrid links; sensing payloads and integrated sensing and communication; onboard computing; storage and caching; and AI-based orchestration. We further synthesize standardization progress, open software and datasets, testbeds, field evidence, and a four-level evaluation methodology ranging from component validation to mission-level effectiveness. An emergency-response case study demonstrates that joint S^2C^2I orchestration substantially improves conjunctive service availability while reducing feeder-link traffic. Finally, we identify research opportunities in agentic AI, trustworthy autonomy, goal-oriented semantic operation and digital twins, and sustainable, certifiable, and open HAP-native systems. The resulting synthesis provides a coherent roadmap from platform design to network-wide deployment.

</details>


### [101] [Payload Swing Estimation and Damping Without Payload Parameters for Multirotor UAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18625)
*K. Taki,K. Umemoto*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出一种仅依赖机载IMU和油门指令的吊挂载荷摆动估计与阻尼方法，通过扩展卡尔曼滤波估计未知摆频并提取周期扰动，利用主动阻尼控制器在姿态环添加修正角度来耗散摆动能，飞行实验验证了在不同绳长和质量变化下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多旋翼无人机吊挂载荷运输灵活但会产生周期性摆动扰动，影响跟踪精度并带来失稳风险。现有抗摆方法需要额外传感器或精确辨识绳长和载荷质量等参数，限制了实际部署。

Method: 使用扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF），将未知的摆动频率作为估计状态，从IMU和油门命令中提取周期性扰动；设计主动阻尼控制器，在姿态环添加修正角度以耗散摆动能。整个方法无需任何载荷参数。

Result: 飞行实验表明，在测试的绳长和质量变化范围内，该方法均能实现鲁棒的摆动阻尼效果。

Conclusion: 仅基于IMU和油门命令即可实现无需参数辨识的摆动估计与阻尼，简化了系统需求，提升了实际现场部署的可行性。

Abstract: Cable-suspended payload transport by multirotor UAVs is flexible but generates periodic swing disturbance that degrades tracking and risks instability. Existing anti-swing methods require additional sensors or precise identification of cable length and payload mass, limiting field deployment. We propose a swing-estimation and damping method using only the onboard IMU and throttle command, requiring no payload parameters. An extended Kalman filter extracts the periodic disturbance with the unknown pendulum frequency as an estimated state, and an active damping controller adds a correction angle to the attitude loop to dissipate pendulum energy. Flight experiments confirm robust damping across a tested range of cable-length and mass variations.

</details>


### [102] [Adaptive Nonlinear Control with Online Identification and Receding-Horizon Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18717)
*Igor Ladnik*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自适应非线性最优控制方案AMIGO，将滚动时域iLQR与状态估计、在线参数辨识及执行器约束相结合，通过三阶段（辨识、规划、闭环控制）组织计算，并利用RK4评估非线性转移、LM方法精化模型参数，在Van der Pol振荡器、四旋翼和月球着陆器三个案例中验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统非线性最优控制方法在面对模型不确定性、参数漂移和执行器约束时表现有限，需要一种能够在线自适应调整模型参数并满足实际约束的鲁棒控制方案。

Method: 将滚动时域iLQR与状态估计、在线参数辨识和执行器约束相结合，构建AMIGO框架；计算过程分为辨识、规划和闭环控制三个时间阶段；使用RK4离散化非线性动态，采用Levenberg-Marquardt方法在线精化模型参数。

Result: 方法在Van der Pol振荡器、四旋翼飞行器和自主月球着陆器下降三个不同动力学场景中得到了成功验证，表明该方法具有良好的泛化能力和实用性。

Conclusion: AMIGO作为一种将参数辨识与最优控制紧密结合的自适应方案，能够有效处理非线性和模型不确定条件下的控制问题，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: An adaptive nonlinear optimal-control scheme is developed by combining receding-horizon iLQR, state estimation, online parameter identification, and actuator constraints. AMIGO (Adaptive Model-based Intelligent Guidance and Orchestration) organizes the computation into three Time Phases: identification, planning, and closed-loop control. The nonlinear transition is evaluated by the fourth-order Runge-Kutta method (RK4), and model parameters are refined by the Levenberg-Marquardt method (LM). The method is illustrated by a Van der Pol oscillator, a quadcopter, and an autonomous lunar-lander descent.

</details>


### [103] [Detecting Feedback-path Delay Injection Attacks Using Interacting Multiple Model Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18824)
*Lovisa Eriksson,Torbjörn Wigren,Dave Zachariah,André M. H. Teixeira*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于交互式多模型（IMM）滤波的变体方法，用于在反馈控制系统中检测延迟注入攻击（尤其在开环隐藏场景下）。通过将延迟视为系统的替代模式来形式化检测方案，并利用平稳分布的理论分析将模型简化为三参数模型，同时指导超参数选择。在巡航控制应用上验证，能在数秒内完成检测且误报率低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络化信息物理系统中，恶意攻击者可以注入时间延迟，对反馈系统产生不利影响。及早检测这类延迟注入攻击是一个重要挑战，目前缺乏有效的早期检测方法。

Method: 提出交互式多模型（IMM）滤波的新变体进行延迟注入攻击检测。将延迟视为系统的替代模式以形式化检测方案；通过平稳分布的理论分析，将模型简化为三参数模型，并据此确定超参数的选择。

Result: 在巡航控制应用上进行评估，方法能够在几秒内检测到延迟注入攻击，且误报概率较低。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够有效、快速地检测反馈控制系统中的延迟注入攻击，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Time-delays are known to have a detrimental effect on feedback systems. In the context of networked cyber-physical systems, delays can be injected by malicious adversaries. Detecting them early is an important challenge. This paper proposes a novel variation of Interacting Multiple Model filtering to detect delay injection attacks in feedback control systems, when hidden in an open loop setting. The detection scheme is formalised by treating delay as alternative modes of the system, and theoretical analysis of the stationary distribution informs a reduction to a three parameter model as well as the choices of hyper parameter values. The method is evaluated on a cruise control application, and shows detection within a few seconds and a low false alarm probability.

</details>


### [104] [Robust Joint Planning of EV and eBus Charging Infrastructure with PV Self-Consumption under Demand Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18844)
*Biswarup Mukherjee*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一个混合整数线性规划（MILP）框架，用于电动汽车（EV）与电动公交（eBus）充电基础设施的联合规划，并考虑光伏（PV）自消耗。模型协同优化充电桩选址、容量配置、技术选型、eBus-车库分配以及逐时充电调度。先构建确定性MILP作为标称基准，再扩展为基于场景的鲁棒最小-最大模型以应对车辆能量需求的不确定性。50节点案例研究表明，线路段eBus需求与PV权重会影响充电桩部署、技术组合及电网购电，而V2G在足够大的惩罚权重下可实现硬可行的鲁棒运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期充电基础设施规划需要同时考虑多样化的车队类型（私家EV与公交eBus）、不同场景下自适应的运行策略以及节点级充电容量约束，现有研究缺乏将充电桩选址、容量、技术选型与运行调度联合优化的统一框架。

Method: 构建确定性MILP标称模型作为基准，再扩展为场景化鲁棒最小-最大模型。鲁棒模型采用共享的第一阶段基础设施决策与场景特定的运行决策，通过epigraph重构最小化基础设施成本加最坏场景运行成本，并使用软可行性惩罚量化未满足充电能量、终端SOC短缺及容量违规。

Result: 50节点案例研究表明，路线段eBus需求与PV加权会显著改变充电桩部署、技术组合与电网购电量；在足够大的惩罚权重下，V2G能够实现硬可行的鲁棒运行。

Conclusion: 有效的长期基础设施规划必须同时考虑多样化车队、适应不同场景的运行策略以及节点级充电容量约束，本文框架为多类车辆协同的充电网络鲁棒规划提供了方法论支撑。

Abstract: This paper presents a mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) framework for joint electric vehicle (EV) and electric bus (eBus) charging-infrastructure planning with photovoltaic (PV) self-consumption. The model co-optimizes charger siting, sizing, technology selection, eBus-to-depot assignment, and hourly charging schedules. A deterministic MILP is first formulated as a nominal benchmark and then extended to a scenario-based robust min--max formulation under vehicle energy-demand uncertainty. The robust model uses shared first-stage infrastructure decisions and scenario-specific operating decisions, and minimizes infrastructure cost plus the worst-case scenario operating cost through an epigraph reformulation. Soft-feasibility penalties quantify unmet charging energy, terminal state-of-charge (SOC) shortfall, and capacity violations under stressed scenarios. A 50-node case study shows that route-segment eBus demand and PV weighting alter charger deployment, technology mix, and grid import, while V2G enables hard-feasible robust operation at sufficiently large penalty weights. These results highlight that effective long-term infrastructure planning must simultaneously account for diverse vehicle fleets, operational strategies that adapt to different scenarios, and node-level constraints on charging capacity.

</details>


### [105] [Comparing Controller Synthesis Methods with Deadline-Miss Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18850)
*Melanie Gallant,Marc Seidel,Paolo Pazzaglia,Claudio Mandrioli,Christoph Mark,Kevin Schmidt,Frank Allgöwer,Martina Maggio*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文对实时控制系统中处理截止时间超限（deadline overruns）的控制器设计方法进行了比较研究，通过定性评估多种"截止时间感知"控制方法，分析了设计空间中的关键假设，并提供了一个基准比较工具，最后通过Furuta摆和电动机两个案例的仿真验证了不同方法的实际表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有多种处理错失截止时间（deadline misses）的控制设计方法，但这些方法在假设条件、执行语义、运行时信息需求和性能保证等方面存在显著差异，缺乏直接比较的框架，导致研究人员难以评估和选择合适的方法，也难以为新方法提供基准。

Method: 1) 对代表性的"截止时间感知"控制方法进行定性评估；2) 分析控制器的设计空间，识别实时约束、控制设计准则和实现方面的关键假设；3) 揭示不同假设如何导致适用性、控制性能和保证之间的不同权衡；4) 开发一个比较工具，使研究人员能够系统地以最新技术为基准测试新方法；5) 通过Furuta摆和电动机两个案例研究进行仿真验证。

Result: 论文识别并阐明了不同截止时间感知控制方法在假设、设计准则和实现方面的关键差异，揭示了这些差异导致的权衡关系，并通过仿真案例展示了不同方法在错失截止时间条件下的实际行为表现。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了截止时间感知控制方法缺乏系统性比较的空白，提供了设计空间分析框架和比较工具，为研究人员在选择和评估实时控制方法时提供了重要参考，有助于推动该领域的进一步研究与发展。

Abstract: This paper presents a comparative study of real-time controller design methods for dynamical control systems subject to deadline overruns. Numerous techniques have been proposed to handle missed deadlines, i.e., including models or information of possible deadline misses directly in the control design. These approaches substantially differ in their assumptions, supported execution semantics, required runtime information, and provided guarantees, making direct comparison challenging. We address this gap with a qualitative assessment of representative deadline-miss-aware control methods. We analyze the design space of such controllers and identify the critical assumptions on the real-time constraints, the control design criteria and implementation aspects. We expose how these different assumptions lead to the different trade-offs in applicability, and control performance and guarantees. We contribute a comparison tool enabling researchers to systematically benchmark new techniques against the state of the art. The study is supported by simulations on two case studies: a Furuta pendulum and an electric motor to illustrate the practical behavior of the different approaches under missed-deadline conditions.

</details>


### [106] [Deciding Relabeling Observation Consistency in Multi-Agent Discrete-Event Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18866)
*Tomáš Masopust*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了多智能体离散事件系统中可扩展监督者的重标签观测一致性（ROC）问题的可判定性。作者证明ROC在一般情形下是PSPACE完全的（非确定型系统需2个可观测事件和1个不可观测事件，确定型系统需2个可观测和2个不可观测事件），同时给出了多种可多项式时间求解的特例，并指出了文献中一个结构条件的错误并加以修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多智能体离散事件系统中，可扩展监督者通过共同模板控制一群同构智能体。在部分观测条件下，监督者的最大容许性依赖于重标签观测一致性（ROC）条件及其伴随条件。然而ROC是否可判定长期以来是一个开放问题，本文旨在解决这一可判定性问题，并进一步探讨其计算复杂度与可处理特例。

Method: 作者通过归约方法证明ROC的PSPACE完全性：对非确定型系统（2个可观测+1个不可观测事件）和确定型系统（2个可观测+2个不可观测事件）分别构造归约。在可处理方面，作者刻画了保证任意植物均满足ROC的重标签特征，针对不可观测事件上单射的重标签设计了多项式时间算法，并提出了基于饱和与模拟的充分条件及对应多项式测试。此外，作者研究了ROC的组合性性质，并修正了文献中错误的结构条件。

Result: ROC判定问题在一般情形下是PSPACE完全的；对于模板对不相交可构成"成分"的系统，ROC具有组合性（字母表也两两不相交时等价性成立）；文献中保障ROC的结构条件被证明有误，作者给出了修正后的伴随包含关系。

Conclusion: 本文完整解决了ROC可判定性的开放问题，划定了其计算复杂度的边界（PSPACE完全），并提供了多条实用的多项式时间可处理路径，同时纠正了文献中的错误，为多智能体离散事件系统部分观测下的可扩展监督设计提供了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: Scalable supervisors for multi-agent discrete-event systems control groups of isomorphic agents through a common template. Under partial observation, such a supervisor is maximally permissive if the relabeling that maps the agents onto their template is relabeling observation consistent (ROC) and a local companion condition holds. Whether ROC is decidable was open. We show that it is PSPACE-complete: for nondeterministic plants with two observable and one unobservable event, and for deterministic plants with two observable and two unobservable events. On the tractable side, we characterize the relabelings that guarantee ROC for every plant, we give a polynomial-time algorithm for deterministic plants whose relabeling is injective on unobservable events, and we give sufficient conditions based on saturation and on simulation, with polynomial-time tests for both. We further show that ROC is compositional for components with pairwise disjoint templates, with equivalence if the alphabets are moreover pairwise disjoint, and that neither hypothesis can be dropped. Finally, we show that the structural condition proposed in the literature to guarantee ROC is incorrect, and repair the companion inclusion that the same proposition asserts.

</details>


### [107] [Limitations of RMS-based Stability Assessment of Converter-based Grids: A Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18989)
*Pol Jane-Soneira,Ognjen Stanojev,Francisco Canales Perez,Qian Long,Mario Schweizer,Matthias Biskoping*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文通过工业电网案例研究，揭示了传统RMS模型在评估换流器主导电网稳定性时的根本缺陷，并提出了一种在旋转dq参考系中保留电磁动态微分方程的替代建模方法，该方法能正确识别不稳定模态，与EMT仿真结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着电力电子换流器在工业电网中的渗透率不断提高，系统在高频段（远超传统电力系统机电暂态范围）出现稳定性挑战。传统商业软件基于RMS模型的特征值分析假设网络准稳态，忽略了资产电磁动态，可能导致错误的稳定性判断——即预测系统稳定而实际系统不稳定。

Method: 提出一种在旋转dq参考坐标系中构建网络动态模型的方法，保留电磁动态的微分方程，从而能够通过特征值分析识别高频不稳定模态。该方法与详细EMT仿真对比验证。

Result: 案例研究表明：传统RMS特征值分析预测系统稳定，但EMT仿真显示存在增长振荡（不稳定）。所提出的dq框架动态阻抗模型能够正确识别不稳定模态，与EMT仿真结果一致。

Conclusion: RMS模型不适用于换流器主导电网的稳定性评估，而dq框架中的动态阻抗模型为涵盖高频换流器-网络交互的精确小信号分析提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The increasing penetration of power electronic converters in industrial grids introduces stability challenges at frequencies well above the electromechanical range of traditional power systems. Commercial software tools typically perform eigenvalue-based stability analysis using root-mean-square (RMS) models that assume a quasi-stationary network, thereby neglecting electromagnetic dynamics of assets --- which is standard practice in power system analysis. This paper presents a case study of an industrial grid where the conventional RMS-based eigenvalue analysis predicts stable operation, while a detailed electromagnetic transient (EMT) simulation reveals growing oscillations, indicating instability. To bridge this gap, we introduce an alternative modeling approach that formulates the network dynamics in the rotating $dq$ reference frame, retaining the differential equations of electromagnetic dynamics. The resulting model enables eigenvalue analysis that correctly identifies the unstable modes, consistent with the EMT simulation results. Our findings highlight a fundamental limitation of RMS-based stability assessment for converter-dominated grids and demonstrate that dynamic impedance models in the dq frame provide a viable path toward accurate small-signal analysis encompassing high-frequency converter-network interactions.

</details>

### [108] [The Market's Conditioning Representation: Equilibrium, Crowding, and Convention Multiplicity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18299)
*Alejandro Rodriguez Dominguez*

Main category: q-fin.PM

TL;DR: 本文提出表征均衡（representation equilibrium）框架，将资产定价中的条件信息集内生化为市场参与者可选择的表征结构。资本在表征间的配置决定聚合头寸与清算溢价，价格反馈又通过因果认证改变表征价值与可行域，形成配置-价格-认证的固定点循环。该框架区分了头寸拥挤（市场冲击）与表征拥挤（驱动空间重叠），并在风险容量约束下刻画了三种位置路径状态：唯一解、仅无创新偏离、以及连续统自证惯例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统资产定价模型依赖固定信息集作为外生假设，无法解释市场价格如何内生塑造投资者对信息的表征方式。本文旨在将市场的条件架构内生化，理解表征选择如何通过价格反馈影响均衡，以及表征拥挤（而非单纯的头寸拥挤）如何产生价格偏离和多元惯例。

Method: 构建配置-价格-认证闭环的表征均衡模型：资本在多种表征间分配决定聚合头寸与清算溢价；价格反馈改变表征价值并经由因果认证决定可容许表征集合。采用紧性条件下的预解式正则性与连续认证证明均衡存在性；在稳定认证胞内利用凹人口博弈与小增益条件分析局部唯一性；通过谱统计量（交叉冲击、协方差与部署容量）划分三种位置路径状态。

Result: 在稳定认证胞内，谱统计量阈值决定三种状态：低于1/2时基本解唯一；临界点仅存在无创新偏离；高于阈值时失稳方向支撑连续统的自证惯例。单调冲击效应位于唯一性区域内，而无定号冲击效应本身不足以产生多重性。中介对冲可产生无定号响应但往返交易仍昂贵；内生风险容量约束惯例振幅；最小二乘学习使主导惯例呈中性而非吸引性。

Conclusion: 表征拥挤为资产定价中的多重均衡和自证预言提供了新的微观基础，独立于传统市场冲击渠道。实证部分不估计阈值，而是记录驱动因子特异的样本外特征以佐证表征拥挤，并明确直接测量所需条件。

Abstract: Asset-pricing models typically condition on a fixed information set. This paper endogenises the market's conditioning architecture by allowing portfolios to choose representations whose induced exposures affect prices. Capital allocated across representations determines aggregate positions and the clearing premium, while price feedback changes representation value and, through causal certification, admissible representations. A representation equilibrium is the fixed point of this configuration--price--certification loop. The framework separates position crowding through market impact from representation crowding generated by driver-space overlap and priced through a basis-invariant information-capacity cost. Under compactness, resolvent regularity and continuous certification, equilibrium exists at every switching cost; within a stable certification cell, informational congestion yields a concave population game and local uniqueness under a small-gain condition. At a settled configuration, a spectral statistic combining cross-impact, covariance and deployed capacity determines three position-path regimes: below one half the fundamental solution is unique; at the boundary only innovation-free deviations remain; above it, destabilising directions support a continuum of self-confirming conventions. Monotone impact lies inside the uniqueness region, while indefinite impact alone is not sufficient for multiplicity. Intermediary hedging can generate an indefinite response while round trips remain costly. Endogenous risk capacity bounds convention amplitudes, and least-squares learning makes the dominant convention neutral rather than attracting. The threshold is not estimated in data; instead, the empirical exercise documents an out-of-sample, driver-specific signature consistent with representation crowding and states the conditions required for direct measurement.

</details>
